あなたは**指摘を反証する役**です。既定は疑う側に置いてください。

判定の詳細（設計根拠の引用・実装参照・根本原因キー）はこちら:
/home/y-saito/Developments/hareruya-design-docs/design_impl_drift_report/design_audit/0408/verdicts.tsv

## 正本（オラクル）

- 設計: /home/y-saito/Developments/hareruya-design-docs/design_impl_drift_report/design_audit/0408/sheets/<シートID>.txt
  （正本HTML設計書 0408 のシート本文を機械抽出したもの。**設計の正はここだけ**）
  レイアウト図は /home/y-saito/Developments/hareruya-design-docs/design_impl_drift_report/design_audit/0408/images/<シートID>_img*.png
- 実装: /home/y-saito/Developments/ec-cube-enterprise （ee）。**実装の正はここだけ**

## 正本でないもの

- /home/y-saito/Developments/pf-eccube3 ほかの現行ソースは移行元であって、eeの正ではない
- Excel設計書（xlsx）は上記シート本文へ変換済み。原本を読む必要はない

## 探してほしいもの（優先順）

### 1. ハルシネーション（最優先）

引用も実装参照も実在するのに、**主張だけが事実と違う**もの。機械検査では捕まりません。
すでに機械で次は確認済みなので、ここを繰り返す必要はありません。

- 設計根拠_引用が正本シート本文に逐語で実在すること（全件すべて実在）
- 実装参照が ee 内に実在するファイル:行であること（全件すべて実在）

疑ってほしいのは次です。

- 「〜していない」と書いてあるが、実は**別の場所でやっている**
  （親クラス・トレイト・イベントリスナ・サブスクライバ・ミドルウェア・
   フォームの制約・バリデータ・DBの制約・Doctrineのライフサイクル）
- 「実装が無い」と書いてあるが、**探し方が悪くて見つけられていない**だけ
- 実装の分岐の読み違い（条件の向き・early return・例外経路）
- 行番号がずれていて、引用した行が主張と関係ない

### 2. 過剰指摘

次は規約で対象外です。指摘になっていたら挙げてください。

- 実装手段の差（通信方式・認可設定・内部配線・クラス名・呼び出し順・join の種類）
- 応答にも画面にも出ない内部挙動
- 現行仕様に書かれた現行ソースの不具合・不用意な副作用（踏襲対象ではない）。
  ただし機能として定義された挙動（警告表示・並び順・据え置き時間等）は要求として扱う
- 現行仕様とリニューアル後の仕様が食い違う箇所は**後者が優先**。現行仕様側は対象外
- 廃止・フェーズ2と正本が宣言しているもの
- 見出し・表の見出し行・区分行・前置き・運用想定・背景説明
- 設計根拠_引用が**仕様を述べていない**もの（項目名・備考・表のヘッダの読み違い）

### 3. 重複

根本原因キーが違っても、実装実態が同じ欠陥を指していれば重複です。
機械で「判定＋実装実態の完全一致」は確認済みです。**文言が違う重複**を探してください。

### 4. 重要度の過大

P1/P2/P3 が業務影響と迂回可否に見合っているか。

## 指摘対象外（これを指摘しても却下します）

- 設計書の書き方・粒度・文体
- 指摘が**少なすぎる**という主張（過小報告の指摘は別途人が見ます）
- 正本シート本文に無いことを根拠にした「設計にこう書くべき」という主張
- MATCHED・OUT_OF_SCOPE・UNVERIFIABLE と判定したものの妥当性
  （成果物には載っていません。本レビューの範囲外）

## 出力形式

指摘が無ければ `NONE` の1語だけ。

指摘ごとに次を書いてください。

```
要求ID:
判定: ハルシネーション | 過剰指摘 | 重複 | 重要度過大
主張: 成果物が何と言っているか
実際: 実装の file:line を必ず示す。何行目に何と書いてあるか
修正案: 取り下げ / 判定をこう変える / 重要度をこうする / どの要求IDへ畳む
```

**重要度過大と判断したときは、修正案に「P3へ変更する」のように正しい重要度を必ず書いてください。**
書かないと修正が適用されず、重要度は過大なまま残ります。
**重複と判断したときは、修正案に畳む先の要求ID（sheet-N-RNNN）を必ず書いてください。**

name: design-impl-drift-audit
description: Compare HTML design specifications against an implementation to find functional drift and produce an evidence-backed HTML report. # Design Impl Drift Audit

## Purpose

Use this skill to audit functional differences between HTML design specifications and an implementation repository, with the design docs treated as authoritative. Produce or continue a report that lists concrete differences with design locations, implementation locations, confidence, severity, and impact.

## Repository Discovery

1. Find the design-doc directory and implementation directory from the user request. If omitted, infer from nearby names such as `*-design-docs` and application repos.
2. Look for an existing report before starting new work. Prefer directories named `design_impl_drift_report`, existing `findings.json`, per-function `findings/*.json`, and `index.html`.
3. Count total target specs and audited specs so the report clearly states scope and remaining work.
4. Preserve existing findings. Append or refine; do not overwrite unrelated prior work.

## Audit Workflow

For each target HTML spec:

1. Identify the feature ID, route/action name, UI template, CSV/batch/API behavior, and acceptance rules from the HTML design spec.
2. Extract a requirement inventory from the HTML spec before looking at code. This inventory must include not only route/screen names, but every concrete requirement in 処理概要, 図形・テキストボックス, 項目表, 表示メッセージ, 業務ルール・計算, 入力項目, エッジケース, データ整合性, API/バッチ結果, 入出力, DB操作, バリデーション, 権限・認可, 画面遷移, エラー処理, ログ・監査, セッション, Cookie, and 排他制御. Treat bullets such as "押下時", "場合", "登録/更新/保存", "遷移", "保持", "表示/非表示", "表示切り替え", "エリアを表示", "誘導", "エラー", "判定", "連携", time limits, thresholds, and retry/idempotency rules as auditable requirements. 秒後/分後, `画面を表示してから`, 自動遷移, タイマー, and store/kiosk/account-specific forced redirects are material requirements even if the route and template already exist.
3. For every extracted requirement, create or mentally maintain a `requirementTrace` row with: `requirementId`, `designRef`, `designRequirement`, `implementationSearchTerms`, `implementationRefs`, `implementationActual`, `traceVerdict` (`MATCHED|DRIFT|NOT_FOUND|UNCERTAIN|PHASE2_EXCLUDED`), and `traceReason`. A feature cannot be marked no-drift until all material requirements have a trace verdict other than `NOT_FOUND` or unresolved `UNCERTAIN`.
4. Search the implementation with `rg` across likely files: controllers, services, repositories, form types, entities, migrations, Twig/templates, commands/batches, route definitions, JavaScript, CSV exporters/importers, and PDF/print services. Search by business terms from the requirement, route names, translation keys, DB columns, state names, constants, statuses, time values, thresholds, and side-effect verbs. For timer or auto-navigation requirements, explicitly search client and server paths for `setTimeout`, `setInterval`, millisecond/second constants such as `60000` and `60 * 1000`, `location.href`, `location.assign`, `location.replace`, redirect routes, and the condition that limits the behavior. For a negative result, also search nearby implemented flows so the report can explain why they do not satisfy the design.
5. Compare behavior and user-facing text, not just names. Check input fields, validation, defaults, search conditions, session persistence, buttons/actions, labels, button text, messages, translation keys/locales, permissions, status transitions, CSV columns, sort/order rules, scheduled timing, timeouts/expiration, thresholds, client-side timers, forced auto-navigation, side effects, idempotency, external integrations, conditional display toggles, alternate UI areas, and error handling.
6. Do not treat "route/controller/template exists" as sufficient. Existence only satisfies the route/screen requirement. It does not satisfy individual rules inside the screen, such as timeout handling, cart retention, validation branches, status transitions, DB writes, logging prohibitions, conditional hide/show behavior, alternative contact/help areas, or external API retry behavior.
7. For conditional display requirements, audit each side of the condition separately. If the HTML says "Aの場合はXを非表示、Yを表示 / A以外はXを表示、Yを非表示", record trace rows for the condition detector, X visibility, Y visibility, and the inverse condition. A nearby notice or route is not enough unless the cited template/JS actually renders the required area under the required condition.
8. For labels, button text, messages, and translation keys, do a dedicated near-match pass. Extract user-visible Japanese text from the HTML item/message tables and compare it with implementation locale values and Twig/Form usage. Treat punctuation, okurigana, missing characters, long-vowel marks, wording changes such as `商品を追加` vs `商品の追加`, and business-term changes such as `返金` vs `送金` as audit targets.
9. A text drift finding is complete only when it cites both the translation/source value and the display path that uses it, such as `messages.ja.yaml` plus the `FormType` label or Twig `|trans` call. If the implementation value exists but no display path is verified, mark it `UNCERTAIN` instead of presenting it as confirmed.
10. Do not stamp all functions as if every standard aspect was fully checked. Separate "standard audit target aspects" from actual evidence. For each finding, list the aspects actually supported by evidence; for no-drift functions, include a requirement trace summary naming the concrete HTML requirements checked and implementation refs searched.
11. Record only actionable drift. If evidence is incomplete, mark the finding `UNCERTAIN` and explain what remains ambiguous.
12. For every drift finding, split the result into implementation-ready fields: `designExpectation`, `implementationActual`, `comparisonRows`, `comparisonSummary`, `mismatchReason`, `impact`, `fixTarget`, `requiredChange`, `implementationRefs`, and, when available, short design/implementation snippets from the cited lines. Do not rely on a generic prose paragraph alone.
13. Each `comparisonRows` entry must answer all four questions explicitly: comparison item, exact design value/requirement, exact implementation value/behavior, and why those two do not match. If any of those four cells would be vague, continue investigating before recording the finding.
14. Treat phase labels such as Ph2 as auxiliary context. They can be stored, but the main report must not use phase status as a substitute for concrete behavioral differences and exact implementation locations.
15. If the HTML design spec explicitly says the whole function or scoped subfeature is `フェーズ2で実装する`, `Ph2で対応`, or `Ph1では実装しない`, do not report absence of that planned Phase 2 work as drift or as a clear implementation gap. You may keep a secondary audit note that non-implementation was confirmed, but exclude it from finding counts and implementation-gap counts. Only keep a finding when there is a concrete non-phase mismatch, such as an implemented Phase 1 path behaving differently from the design or user-facing text differing.
16. When no drift is found, add the function to the audited set with an empty findings list only after the requirement trace gate passes. If material HTML requirements are untraced, do not mark `AUDITED_NO_DRIFT_FOUND`; mark the function as requiring review or add `UNCERTAIN` findings with the unresolved requirements.
17. Do not let one confirmed finding hide other requirements in the same function. A function with `AUDITED_WITH_FINDINGS` can still have untraced material requirements; keep those requirements visible in the trace gate until each one is converted to a confirmed finding, an uncertain finding, a matched trace row, or a documented phase/design non-implementation exclusion.
18. For explicitly registered critical requirements, the harness should carry a missing-finding template or an equivalent escalation rule. If the trace is not covered by an existing finding, promote it into findings automatically or fail the quality gate loudly. Do not rely on a hand-maintained `ADDITIONAL_FINDINGS` list as the only path for user-reported or high-risk missing behavior such as timeouts, cart retention, one-minute/30-minute limits, store-account auto redirects, conditional UI areas, status transitions, external calls, or destructive side effects.
19. The HTML report must not expose broad source-signal candidates as if they were implementation gaps. A requirement-level list in the main report must be limited to confirmed or explicitly uncertain actionable drift derived from findings, such as `NOT_FOUND` or `DRIFT` requirement traces and concrete comparison rows. If a source-signal scan is used for triage, keep it out of the main HTML report unless each row has been verified against implementation source and written as a concrete finding.
20. Do not hide time-based or automatic-navigation requirements merely because they are not confirmed findings yet. Requirements involving `画面を表示してから`, seconds/minutes after display, auto redirect/navigation, or timeout-to-cart/top behavior should appear in a separate focused untraced-requirement audit queue when they remain uncovered. This queue is not a confirmed defect count; it exists so these high-risk requirements are reviewed against Controller/Twig/JavaScript/Service source instead of being buried among broad requirement-trace failures.

## Function-Level Implementation Gap Audit

For every HTML spec sheet, maintain a function-level implementation audit even when no drift finding is confirmed yet.

- Extract implementation search terms from the HTML design: route names, URL paths, translation keys, class/controller/service names, batch/API/CSV names, and high-signal business terms from the sheet title and requirement text.
- Search implementation source by function across Controller, Form, Service, Repository, Entity, Migration, Twig, Command/Batch, CSV, PDF/print, API/webhook, locale/messages, JavaScript, and configuration files.
- Store a `functionImplementationAudit` object with `verdict`, `designDerivedTerms`, `candidateRefs`, `candidateCount`, `strongSignalCount`, `searchedAreas`, and `auditNote`.
- Use separate verdicts:
  - `CONFIRMED_IMPLEMENTATION_GAP`: a concrete drift finding already proves missing implementation.
  - `NO_IMPLEMENTATION_ENTRY_FOUND`: no entry candidate was found from design-derived terms; treat as an additional-review candidate, not an automatic confirmed defect.
  - `IMPLEMENTATION_ENTRY_FOUND`: strong source evidence such as route, class, translation key, or path was found.
  - `IMPLEMENTATION_CANDIDATE_FOUND`: weak business-term candidates were found but the entry point is not proven.
  - `PHASE2_NONIMPLEMENTATION_EXPECTED`: HTML design explicitly scopes non-implementation to Phase 2/Ph1 exclusion.
  - `DESIGN_NONIMPLEMENTATION_EXPECTED`: HTML design explicitly says the function or scoped behavior is not implemented by code, such as `実装しない`, `実装不要`, or infrastructure/external-service configuration replacing application code.
- Use `DESIGN_NONIMPLEMENTATION_EXPECTED` as the function-level verdict only when the non-implementation statement applies to the whole function or the implementation entry itself, for example an external WordPress server, Docker image cron, Aurora parameter group, or a bare whole-sheet `実装しない`. If the text only excludes a scoped subfeature, UI alert, HTML hint, logging detail, partial Phase 2 item, or internal job, keep the function's source-entry verdict and mention the scoped exclusion only in the trace/audit note.
- The report must show both confirmed implementation-gap findings and function-level no-entry candidates. Do not merge these counts: implementers need confirmed defects for fixing and no-entry candidates for review planning.
- A function-level candidate is not enough to mark the detailed requirements as implemented. Requirement trace still controls no-drift status.
- Do not set report metadata such as `auditComplete` or narrative notes to "complete" merely because every sheet has a function-level implementation audit. If any material requirement trace remains unresolved, state that all sheets have function-entry coverage but requirement-level audit is incomplete.
- For infrastructure-oriented sheets such as Step Functions, Lambda, WAF/S3 jobs, cron configured outside the application, or managed database settings, distinguish "not found in the requested implementation repository" from "not implemented anywhere". A confirmed implementation-gap finding may be scoped to the audited repository, but the report must say when external infrastructure repositories or cloud configuration were not checked.

## Requirement Trace Gate

Before marking a function no-drift, verify the following:

- Every material HTML requirement has a trace row. Material means it changes behavior, data, display, validation, permission, transition, side effect, external communication, schedule, retry/idempotency, logging/audit, CSV/API/PDF/batch I/O, or user-visible text.
- Each trace row includes exact design text or a tight paraphrase plus line/anchor reference, not only the sheet title.
- Each matched row cites the implementation path and line(s) that actually execute or render the behavior. A nearby class, route, or template is not enough unless the cited code contains the relevant condition/value/side effect.
- Each missing row is converted to a confirmed or uncertain finding. Use confirmed only after searching the direct path and nearby alternative implementations.
- Missing rows remain failures even if the function already has other findings. The gate is requirement-scoped, not function-scoped.
- If an explicit critical requirement has a configured missing-finding template, regenerate the report from the harness and verify the promoted finding appears in both JSON and HTML with design value, implementation behavior, mismatch reason, fix target, and search evidence.
- If a missing row does not yet have enough evidence for a confirmed or uncertain actionable finding, do not count it as an implementation gap and do not put it in the main HTML fix list. Keep the function in an incomplete requirement-trace state, and promote the row only after implementation source has been checked enough to state the exact design requirement, exact implementation behavior, and mismatch reason.
- A generic `auditEvidence` string is not a substitute for requirement traces.
- Negative searches must list the searched terms/areas and why nearby hits do not satisfy the design.

## Finding Format

Store findings in a machine-readable JSON structure. Use this shape for each finding:

```json
{
  "id": "stable-feature-id-001",
  "functionId": "m04-01_admin_stock_stock_search_list",
  "functionName": "在庫一覧",
  "severity": "high|med|low",
  "dimension": "①機能/ルート未実装",
  "confidence": "CONFIRMED|UNCERTAIN",
  "design": {
    "file": "path/to/spec.html",
    "lines": "215-220",
    "summary": "設計書に書かれている仕様"
  },
  "implementation": {
    "file": "path/to/source.php or 不在",
    "lines": "337-344",
    "summary": "実装側の状態"
  },
  "difference": "設計と実装の具体的な差分",
  "comparisonRows": [
    {
      "item": "比較項目名",
      "design": "設計書の値・要求。列名、HTTPメソッド、パス、保存先、表示文言、条件、状態遷移などを具体値で書く",
      "implementation": "実装の値・挙動。実際の列名、メソッド、パス、保存先、文言、条件、処理順を具体値で書く",
      "mismatch": "なぜ一致しないのか。実装者が直すべき差分を一文で書く"
    }
  ],
  "requirementTrace": [
    {
      "requirementId": "stable-requirement-id",
      "designRef": "path/to/spec.html:123",
      "designRequirement": "HTML設計書の個別要求",
      "implementationSearchTerms": ["検索語", "route_name", "methodName"],
      "implementationRefs": ["src/...php:123"],
      "implementationActual": "実装で確認できた挙動、または不在判定",
      "traceVerdict": "MATCHED|DRIFT|NOT_FOUND|UNCERTAIN|PHASE2_EXCLUDED",
      "traceReason": "なぜその判定か"
    }
  ],
  "comparisonSummary": "設計はX、実装はY、したがってZが違う",
  "mismatchReason": "乖離と判断した具体的な理由",
  "impact": "ユーザー・業務・データへの影響",
  "fixTarget": "修正対象のファイル/ルート/サービス/不在箇所",
  "requiredChange": "設計に合わせる場合に必要な修正",
  "implementationRefs": ["src/...php:123"],
  "implementationSnippets": ["123: 実装コードの短い断片"],
  "evidence": ["短い根拠。ファイル名と行番号を含める"]
}
```

The HTML report should make these fields visible as separate blocks so an implementer can answer: what exact value does the design require, what exact value does the code implement, why is that a mismatch, exactly where is the code, and what should be changed.

Use stable IDs and Japanese text when the existing report is Japanese. Keep dimensions consistent with existing findings when continuing a report.

## Severity Guidance

- `high`: missing feature/route, wrong critical business rule, destructive or externally visible behavior, missing required import/export/batch/PDF flow.
- `med`: partial implementation, wrong filtering/sorting/session behavior, missing non-critical columns or validation, incompatible screen behavior.
- `low`: copy/display/default-state differences, ambiguous design wording, minor workflow variance.

## Report Generation

If the project already has a report generator, use it. Otherwise copy or adapt `scripts/build_report.py` from this skill into the report directory, then run it from the repository root or pass paths as needed.

The HTML report must include:

- summary counts: total findings, severities, audited functions, functions with drift
- scope and remaining work
- per-function sections
- each finding with severity, confidence, dimension, audit aspects including label/message/translation-key differences when applicable, design reference, implementation reference, design expectation, implementation actual behavior, `comparisonRows`, mismatch reason, impact, fix target, required change, and evidence
- a comparison table with columns: 比較項目 / 設計書の値・要求 / 実装の値・挙動 / 乖離理由
- links or file paths precise enough for a developer to inspect
- short cited design and implementation snippets when available
- phase/Ph2 data only as hidden or secondary metadata, never as a report column or primary summary
- a function-level implementation audit section showing verdict counts, no-entry candidates, candidate source references, and the search terms that produced them
- a requirement-level difference list derived only from confirmed/uncertain findings, showing the exact design requirement, implementation behavior, mismatch reason, implementation refs, and a link back to the finding. Do not include broad unverified source-signal queues in the main HTML report.
- a focused untraced-requirement section for small, high-risk classes such as display-time timers, automatic redirects, and timeout navigation. Keep it separate from confirmed difference and implementation-gap counts, and include search terms/candidate refs so the next audit step is concrete.

After editing findings, validate JSON and regenerate HTML:

```bash
python3 -m json.tool path/to/design_impl_drift_report/findings.json
python3 path/to/design_impl_drift_report/build_report.py
```

## Quality Bar

- Treat the HTML design spec as source of truth even when implementation looks intentional.
- Cite exact file paths and line numbers whenever available.
- Distinguish `不在` from “not found yet”; use `不在` only after searching likely implementation areas.
- Avoid broad claims such as “not implemented” without search evidence or contrasting nearby implemented features.
- Do not classify Phase 2 planned non-implementation as a defect. A missing route, CSV, batch, API, table, or screen is only a drift finding when the design expects it in the audited implementation phase.
- For label/message drift, prefer exact line evidence and a verified render path over fuzzy string similarity. Fuzzy or near-match scans are candidate generators; they are not findings until source and display usage are confirmed.
- When an external reviewer or another agent reports candidates, verify each candidate against the HTML design and implementation source before adding it. Keep rejected or ambiguous candidates out of confirmed counts, or mark them `UNCERTAIN` with the unresolved reason.
- For absent implementations, include the searched terms/areas, nearby implementation files with line numbers, and a concrete reason those nearby files do not satisfy the design. Do not leave `implRef` as only `不在` in the final report.
- Reject vague findings. A finding is not complete until it names the exact design value and exact implementation value that differ.
- Keep the report cumulative and resumable.

    45	※ 　ポイント累計は加算ポイント(プラス値)のみ対象とし、失効や使用したポイント(マイナス値)は累計に含めない
    46	・ポイント累計とポイント残高を比較し、ポイント残高の方が多いものを本取得する

    51	プレイヤーデータ.ポイント残高	＞ ポイント履歴データ.ポイント増減量の合計	上記2条件に該当するポイント増減量の合計

    66	・スマレジへのポイント増減API登録失敗(タイムアウト・レスポンスステータスが失敗扱いの場合)
    67	エラーが発生したユーザーのポイント失効処理(スマレジ・ユーザーポイント増減含め)は今回は行わず、次のユーザーの処理を行う
    68	(該当ユーザーのポイント失効処理は翌日以降に持ち越しとなる)

    70	functions/pf-eccube3/b08-02_batch_customer_customer_lost_points.md
    71	現行仕様
    72	B08-02 ポイント失効 / バッチ 会員管理 — ポイント失効
    73	業務ロジック
    74	起動時のバッチ名
    75	起動時に渡されたバッチ名が未指定のとき、または定義済みのバッチ名のいずれとも一致しないときは、失効処理を一切行わない。一致したときだけ失効処理を実行する。
    76	対象会員の絞り込み
    77	スマレジ会員IDが登録されていない会員は、有効期限を過ぎたポイントを持っていても対象に含めない。
    78	有効期限の判定に用いる基準日時は、実行時刻の時分秒を切り捨てて0時0分0秒とする。
    79	会員ごとの処理間隔
    80	対象会員は1件ずつ順に処理し、1件を処理するごとに0.1秒の待ち時間を置いてから次の会員へ進む。
    81	更新の確定
    82	会員ごとの更新はその都度は確定せず、対象会員をすべて処理し終えた時点でまとめて確定する。処理の途中で異常終了した場合、その回の保有ポイントの更新もポイント履歴の記録も一件も残らない。
    83	入出力
    84	起動引数と終了状態
    85	種類	内容
    86	入力	起動時に渡すバッチ名
    87	出力	終了状態。失効処理を実行したときは正常終了、バッチ名が未指定・不一致のときは異常終了とする
    88	リニューアル後の仕様
    89	表示メッセージ
    90	このバッチは画面へ文言を表示しない。

    37	ユーザー情報および履歴合計値を取得し、一覧を作成する

    45	2.　プレイヤーデータ(dtb_player)のポイント残高(point)
    46	・各ユーザーで、上記1と2の結果値が異なっているものだけを抽出

    16	namespace Eccube\Service\Admin\Customer;
    17	
    18	use Doctrine\ORM\EntityManagerInterface;
    19	use Eccube\Entity\DtbPointHistory;
    20	use Eccube\Entity\Master\MtbPointType;
    21	use Eccube\Repository\DtbPointHistoryRepository;
    22	use Eccube\Repository\Master\MtbPointTypeRepository;
    23	use Eccube\Service\EntityManager\PointHistoryEntityManager;
    24	use Eccube\Service\Smaregi\SmaregiCustomerPointEventService;
    25	use Psr\Log\LoggerInterface;
    26	
    27	class LostPointsAction
    28	{
    29	    private const NOTE = '有効期限切れによる失効';
    30	
    31	    public function __construct(
    32	        private readonly DtbPointHistoryRepository $pointHistoryRepository,
    33	        private readonly MtbPointTypeRepository $pointTypeRepository,
    34	        private readonly SmaregiCustomerPointEventService $smaregiCustomerPointEventService,
    35	        private readonly EntityManagerInterface $entityManager,
    36	        private readonly PointHistoryEntityManager $pointHistoryEntityManager,
    37	        private readonly LoggerInterface $logger,
    38	    ) {
    39	    }
    40	
    41	    public function handle(): void
    42	    {
    43	        $lostPointPlayers = $this->pointHistoryRepository->getPlayersForLostPoint();
    44	        $PointType = $this->pointTypeRepository->find(MtbPointType::GRANTED_TYPE);
    45	
    46	        if (empty($lostPointPlayers)) {
    47	            $this->logger->info('ポイント失効処理対象なし');
    48	
    49	            return;
    50	        }
    51	
    52	        foreach ($lostPointPlayers as $lostPointPlayer) {
    53	            $Player = $lostPointPlayer['player'];
    54	            $remainingPoint = (int) $lostPointPlayer['remainingPoint'];
    55	            $losePoint = $remainingPoint - $Player->getPoint();
    56	
    57	            if ($losePoint === 0) {
    58	                // 失効分が無ければ EC・スマレジともに更新不要。
    59	                continue;
    60	            }
    61	
    62	            $LostPointHistory = new DtbPointHistory();
    63	            $job = null;
    64	
    65	            try {
    66	                $this->entityManager->beginTransaction();
    67	
    68	                $Player->setPoint($remainingPoint);
    69	                $this->entityManager->persist($Player);
    70	
    71	                $this->pointHistoryEntityManager->save(
    72	                    PointHistory: $LostPointHistory,
    73	                    Customer: $Player->getCustomer(),
    74	                    Order: null,
    75	                    PointType: $PointType,
    76	                    pointChange: $losePoint,
    77	                    note: self::NOTE,
    78	                    issueDate: (new \DateTime())->modify('+183 days'),
    79	                    transactionId: null,
    80	                );
    81	
    82	                // EC 失効と同一トランザクションでスマレジ連携ジョブを積む（未連携・増減0は null）。
    83	                $job = $this->smaregiCustomerPointEventService->registerPointAddJob($LostPointHistory);
    84	
    85	                $this->entityManager->flush();
    86	                $this->entityManager->commit();
    87	            } catch (\Throwable $e) {
    88	                try {
    89	                    if ($this->entityManager->getConnection()->isTransactionActive()) {
    90	                        $this->entityManager->rollback();
    91	                    }
    92	                } catch (\Throwable) {
    93	                    // ロールバック自体の失敗は握りつぶす（下で打ち切り判定する）。
    94	                }
    95	
    96	                $this->logger->error('ポイント失効処理に失敗しました', [
    97	                    'player_id' => $Player->getId(),
    98	                    'smaregi_id' => $Player->getSmaregiId(),
    99	                    'message' => $e->getMessage(),
   100	                ]);
   101	
   102	                if (!$this->entityManager->isOpen()) {
   103	                    // flush 失敗等で EntityManager が閉じた場合は以降を処理できないため打ち切る（残りは次回実行で再処理）。
   104	                    $this->logger->error('EntityManager が閉じたためポイント失効バッチを中断します（残りは次回実行で再処理）');
   105	
   106	                    break;
   107	                }
   108	
   109	                continue;
   110	            }
   111	
   112	            // commit 成功後にのみスマレジ連携メッセージを dispatch する（アウトボックス的な順序）。
   113	            if ($job !== null) {
   114	                $this->smaregiCustomerPointEventService->dispatchPointAddMessage($job, $LostPointHistory);
   115	            }
   116	        }
   117	    }
   118	}

  2393	            ->returnPath(new Address($this->BaseInfo->getEmail04()))

  2397	        MailUtil::setParameterForCharset($this->eccubeConfig, $message);

  2416	     * Save user mail history

  2419	    {

  2423	            ->setMailBody($message->getTextBody())
  2424	            ->setSendDate(new \DateTime())
  2425	            ->setCreator($creator);
  2426

  2428	            $mailHistory->setMailTemplate($mailTemplate);
  2429	            $mailHistory->setBaseInfo($mailTemplate->getBaseInfo());
  2430	        }
  2431

  2433	            $mailHistory->setCustomer($info);
  2434	        } elseif ($info instanceof Order) {
  2435	            $mailHistory
  2436	                ->setOrder($info)
  2437	                ->setCustomer($info->getCustomer());
  2438	            // ゲストIDを設定
  2439	            $guestId = $info->getGuestId();
  2440	            if ($guestId !== null && $guestId !== '') {
  2441	                $mailHistory->setGuestId($guestId);
  2442	            }
  2443	        } elseif ($info instanceof DtbBuyOrder) {
  2444	            $mailHistory
  2445	                ->setBuyOrder($info)
  2446	                ->setCustomer($info->getCustomer());
  2447	        }
  2448	
  2449	        $this->entityManager->persist($mailHistory);
  2450	        $this->entityManager->flush();

    17	
    18	use Doctrine\ORM\EntityManagerInterface;
    19	use Eccube\Entity\MessengerJob;
    20	use Eccube\Message\SmaregiCustomerPointAddMessage;

    29	
    30	/**
    31	 * 管理画面ポイント付与をスマレジ会員ポイントへ反映する非同期ハンドラ.
    32	 *
    33	 * MessengerJob 行を悲観ロックして重複実行を防ぎ、PENDING/FAILED のときのみ PROCESSING へ遷移する。
    34	 * 失敗時は FAILED にして例外を再送出し、Messenger のリトライに委ねる。
    35	 */
    36	#[AsMessageHandler]
    37	final readonly class SmaregiCustomerPointAddMessageHandler
    38	{
    39	    public function __construct(
    40	        private LoggerInterface $logger,
    41	        private EntityManagerInterface $entityManager,
    42	        private SmaregiMessengerJobProcessingLock $smaregiMessengerJobProcessingLock,
    43	        private MessengerJobRepository $messengerJobRepository,
    44	        private DtbPointHistoryRepository $pointHistoryRepository,
    45	        private SmaregiCustomerService $smaregiCustomerService,
    46	        private SmaregiMessengerJobContext $smaregiMessengerJobContext,
    47	        private SmaregiPointPushLogService $smaregiPointPushLogService,
    48	    ) {
    49	    }
    50	
    51	    public function __invoke(SmaregiCustomerPointAddMessage $message): void
    52	    {
    53	        $Job = $this->messengerJobRepository->find($message->getJobId());
    54	        if (!$Job instanceof MessengerJob) {
    55	            $this->logger->error('MessengerJob not found for SmaregiCustomerPointAddMessage', [
    56	                'jobId' => $message->getJobId(),
    57	                'pointHistoryId' => $message->getPointHistoryId(),
    58	            ]);
    59	
    60	            return;
    61	        }
    62	
    63	        $this->smaregiMessengerJobContext->setJobId((int) $Job->getId());
    64	        try {
    65	            if (!$this->smaregiMessengerJobProcessingLock->tryBeginProcessingJob($Job)) {
    66	                return;
    67	            }
    68	
    69	            $pointHistory = $this->pointHistoryRepository->find($message->getPointHistoryId());
    70	            if ($pointHistory === null) {
    71	                $this->failJob($Job, 'ポイント履歴が見つかりません (pointHistoryId='.$message->getPointHistoryId().').');
    72	
    73	                return;
    74	            }
    75	
    76	            $smaregiId = $pointHistory->getCustomer()->getPlayer()?->getSmaregiId();
    77	            if ($smaregiId === null || $smaregiId === '') {
    78	                $this->completeJobSkipped($Job, 'スマレジ未連携の会員のためポイント連携をスキップしました。');
    79	
    80	                return;
    81	            }
    82	
    83	            $pointChange = $pointHistory->getPointChange();
    84	            if ($pointChange === null || $pointChange === 0) {
    85	                $this->completeJobSkipped($Job, '増減ポイントが 0 のためポイント連携をスキップしました。');
    86	
    87	                return;
    88	            }
    89	
    90	            try {
    91	                $response = $this->smaregiCustomerService->postSmaregiPoint($smaregiId, $pointChange);
    92	            } catch (\Throwable $e) {
    93	                // postSmaregiPoint は通常例外を投げないが、想定外例外は適用有無が不明なため自動リトライせず手動照合へ回す。
    94	                $this->failJob($Job, 'スマレジ会員ポイント更新で想定外の例外が発生しました（適用有無不明・要手動照合）: '.$e->getMessage());
    95	
    96	                return;
    97	            }
    98	
    99	            if (!array_key_exists('result', $response)) {
   100	                $error = isset($response['error']) && is_string($response['error']) ? $response['error'] : '';
   101	
   102	                // ポイント加算API(point/add)は相対加算で非冪等。加算API実行後（statusCode あり）の失敗は
   103	                // 適用済みの可能性があり、自動リトライすると二重加算になりうるため再送出せず FAILED にして手動照合へ回す。
   104	                if (array_key_exists('statusCode', $response)) {
   105	                    $this->failJob($Job, 'スマレジ会員ポイント更新が不確定な状態で失敗しました（自動リトライ不可・要手動照合）: '.$error);
   106	
   107	                    return;
   108	                }
   109	
   110	                // 加算API実行前の失敗（トークン取得 / customerId 解決）＝外部未適用が確定しているため再送出して再試行する。
   111	                $this->failJob($Job, 'スマレジ会員ポイント更新に失敗しました（外部未適用・再試行）: '.$error);
   112	
   113	                throw new \RuntimeException('スマレジ会員ポイント更新に失敗しました（外部未適用・再試行）: '.$error);
   114	            }
   115	
   116	            // ここに到達した時点でスマレジ側のポイント加算は成功している。
   117	            // echo 抑止の送信記録(persist)と COMPLETED を同一 flush で確定する。ここでの「失敗」は DB 永続化(flush)の失敗を指し、
   118	            // その場合はどちらも残さない（原子的）。取引ID無しは失敗ではない（成功で取引ID無しは postSmaregiPoint でエラー化、skipped は取引なしで記録不要）。
   119	            // point/add は非冪等のため、この flush 失敗では再送出（リトライ）せずログのみとする（echo 抑止が DB に残らない恐れ・要手動照合）。
   120	            try {
   121	                // この point/add がスマレジ側に生む取引が Webhook で戻ってきても二重処理しないよう、取引IDを送信記録へ積む。
   122	                $this->smaregiPointPushLogService->recordFromResult($response);
   123	                $Job->setStatus(MessengerJob::STATUS_COMPLETED);
   124	                $Job->setCompletedAt(new \DateTime());
   125	                $Job->setErrorMessage(null);
   126	                $this->entityManager->flush();
   127	            } catch (\Throwable $persistError) {
   128	                $this->logger->error('Smaregi customer point applied but failed to persist push log/COMPLETED state', [
   129	                    'jobId' => $Job->getId(),
   130	                    'error' => $persistError->getMessage(),
   131	                ]);
   132	            }
   133	        } finally {
   134	            $this->smaregiMessengerJobContext->clear();
   135	        }
   136	    }
   137	
   138	    private function failJob(MessengerJob $Job, string $errorMessage): void
   139	    {
   140	        $Job->setStatus(MessengerJob::STATUS_FAILED);
   141	        $Job->setCompletedAt(new \DateTime());
   142	        $Job->setErrorMessage($errorMessage);
   143	        try {
   144	            $this->entityManager->flush();
   145	        } catch (\Throwable $persistError) {
   146	            $this->logger->error('Failed to persist Smaregi customer point job failure state', [
   147	                'jobId' => $Job->getId(),
   148	                'error' => $persistError->getMessage(),
   149	            ]);
   150	        }
   151	        $this->logger->error($errorMessage, ['jobId' => $Job->getId()]);
   152	    }
   153	
   154	    private function completeJobSkipped(MessengerJob $Job, string $reason): void
   155	    {
   156	        $Job->setStatus(MessengerJob::STATUS_COMPLETED);
   157	        $Job->setCompletedAt(new \DateTime());
   158	        $Job->setErrorMessage(null);
   159	        try {
   160	            $this->entityManager->flush();
   161	        } catch (\Throwable $persistError) {
   162	            $this->logger->error('Failed to persist Smaregi customer point job skipped state', [
   163	                'jobId' => $Job->getId(),
   164	                'error' => $persistError->getMessage(),
   165	            ]);
   166	        }
   167	        $this->logger->info($reason, ['jobId' => $Job->getId()]);
   168	    }
   169	}

     1	<?php
     2	
     3	declare(strict_types=1);
     4	
     5	/*
     6	 * This file is part of EC-CUBE
     7	 *
     8	 * Copyright(c) EC-CUBE CO.,LTD. All Rights Reserved.
     9	 *
    10	 * http://www.ec-cube.co.jp/

    15	
    16	namespace Eccube\Service\Smaregi;
    17	
    18	use Doctrine\ORM\EntityManagerInterface;
    19	use Eccube\Entity\DtbPointHistory;
    20	use Eccube\Entity\MessengerJob;
    21	use Eccube\Message\SmaregiCustomerPointAddMessage;
    22	use Psr\Log\LoggerInterface;
    23	use Symfony\Component\Messenger\MessageBusInterface;
    24	
    25	/**
    26	 * 管理画面ポイント付与をスマレジ会員ポイントへ非同期反映するための連携ジョブを扱う.
    27	 *
    28	 * EC のポイント履歴登録と同一トランザクションで MessengerJob を INSERT し（{@see registerPointAddJob()}）、
    29	 * commit 成功後に {@see dispatchPointAddMessage()} でメッセージを送る（アウトボックス的な順序）。
    30	 */
    31	class SmaregiCustomerPointEventService
    32	{
    33	    public function __construct(
    34	        private readonly MessageBusInterface $messageBus,
    35	        private readonly EntityManagerInterface $entityManager,
    36	        private readonly LoggerInterface $logger,
    37	    ) {
    38	    }
    39	
    40	    /**
    41	     * ポイント付与と同一 DB トランザクションで連携ジョブを積む（flush は呼び出し側）.
    42	     *
    43	     * スマレジ未連携（smaregi_id 無し）や増減 0 の場合は連携不要のため null を返す。
    44	     */
    45	    public function registerPointAddJob(DtbPointHistory $pointHistory): ?MessengerJob
    46	    {
    47	        $smaregiId = $pointHistory->getCustomer()->getPlayer()?->getSmaregiId();
    48	        if ($smaregiId === null || $smaregiId === '') {
    49	            return null;
    50	        }
    51	
    52	        $pointChange = $pointHistory->getPointChange();
    53	        if ($pointChange === null || $pointChange === 0) {
    54	            return null;
    55	        }
    56	
    57	        $job = new MessengerJob();
    58	        $job->setMessageClass(SmaregiCustomerPointAddMessage::class);
    59	        $job->setStatus(MessengerJob::STATUS_PENDING);
    60	        $job->setPayloadSummary(sprintf('smaregiId=%s point=%d', $smaregiId, $pointChange));
    61	        $this->entityManager->persist($job);
    62	
    63	        return $job;
    64	    }
    65	
    66	    /**
    67	     * registerPointAddJob の flush / commit 成功後にのみ呼ぶ。
    68	     * メッセージバス投入に失敗しても例外は投げず、ジョブを FAILED にしてログする（EC 側処理は既に確定済み）。
    69	     */
    70	    public function dispatchPointAddMessage(MessengerJob $job, DtbPointHistory $pointHistory): void
    71	    {
    72	        $jobId = (int) $job->getId();
    73	        if ($jobId < 1) {
    74	            $this->logger->error('Smaregi customer point job has no id; cannot dispatch message bus.', [
    75	                'pointHistoryId' => $pointHistory->getId(),
    76	            ]);
    77	
    78	            return;
    79	        }
    80	
    81	        try {
    82	            $this->messageBus->dispatch(new SmaregiCustomerPointAddMessage($pointHistory->getId(), $jobId));
    83	        } catch (\Throwable $e) {
    84	            try {
    85	                $managed = $this->entityManager->find(MessengerJob::class, $jobId);
    86	                if ($managed instanceof MessengerJob) {
    87	                    $managed->setStatus(MessengerJob::STATUS_FAILED);
    88	                    $managed->setCompletedAt(new \DateTime());
    89	                    $managed->setErrorMessage('メッセージバスへの投入に失敗しました: '.$e->getMessage());
    90	                    $this->entityManager->flush();
    91	                }
    92	            } catch (\Throwable $persistError) {
    93	                // FAILED 永続化にも失敗したためジョブは PENDING のまま残る。再回収できるよう業務IDも記録する。
    94	                $this->logger->error('Failed to persist Smaregi customer point job failure state', [
    95	                    'jobId' => $jobId,
    96	                    'pointHistoryId' => $pointHistory->getId(),
    97	                    'error' => $persistError->getMessage(),
    98	                ]);
    99	            }
   100	            $this->logger->error('Smaregi customer point add message dispatch failed', [
   101	                'exception' => $e,
   102	                'jobId' => $jobId,
   103	                'pointHistoryId' => $pointHistory->getId(),
   104	            ]);
   105	        }
   106	    }
   107	}

   621	    public function findMostAppositeOneByEmail(string $email): ?Customer
   622	    {
   623	        $qb = $this->createQueryBuilder('c')
   624	            ->addSelect('(CASE WHEN s.id = :active_customer THEN 1 ELSE 0 END) AS HIDDEN active_status')
   625	            ->innerJoin('c.Status', 's')

   630	            ->setParameter('email', $email)
   631	            ->setParameter('active_customer', CustomerStatus::REGULAR)
   632	            ->setParameter('del_flg', 0)
   633	            ->setMaxResults(1);
   634	
   635	        return $qb->getQuery()->getOneOrNullResult();
   636	    }

   643	    public function getBlankRequiredItemCustomers(): array

   655	                ':postal_code:', COALESCE(c.postal_code, 'null')) AS info",
   656	            ])
   657	            ->leftJoin('c.Country', 'co')
   658	            ->leftJoin('c.Pref', 'p')
   659	            ->andWhere('c.delFlg = :delFlg')
   660	            ->andWhere($expr->orX(
   661	                $expr->isNull('c.Country'),
   662	                $expr->isNull('c.Pref'),
   663	                $expr->isNull('c.name01'),

     1	<?php
     2	
     3	declare(strict_types=1);
     4	
     5	/*
     6	 * This file is part of EC-CUBE
     7	 *
     8	 * Copyright(c) EC-CUBE CO.,LTD. All Rights Reserved.
     9	 *
    10	 * http://www.ec-cube.co.jp/
    11	 *
    12	 * For the full copyright and license information, please view the LICENSE
    13	 * file that was distributed with this source code.
    14	 */
    15	
    16	namespace Eccube\Service\Admin\Customer;
    17	
    18	use Doctrine\ORM\EntityManagerInterface;
    19	use Eccube\Repository\DtbPointHistoryRepository;
    20	use Eccube\Service\MailService;
    21	use Psr\Log\LoggerInterface;
    22	
    23	class AdjustPointVarianceAction
    24	{
    25	    public function __construct(
    26	        private readonly DtbPointHistoryRepository $pointHistoryRepository,
    27	        private readonly EntityManagerInterface $entityManager,
    28	        private readonly MailService $mailService,
    29	        private readonly LoggerInterface $logger,
    30	    ) {
    31	    }
    32	
    33	    public function handle(): void
    34	    {
    35	        $pointDifferentials = $this->pointHistoryRepository->getCustomersWithPointDifferential();
    36	
    37	        if (empty($pointDifferentials)) {
    38	            $this->logger->info('ポイント差分発生対象なし');
    39	
    40	            return;
    41	        }
    42	
    43	        $pointVarianceList = [];
    44	
    45	        try {
    46	            $this->entityManager->beginTransaction();
    47	
    48	            foreach ($pointDifferentials as $pointDifferential) {
    49	                $player = $pointDifferential['player'];
    50	                $customerId = $player->getCustomer()->getId();
    51	                $pointHistoryTotal = (int) $pointDifferential['point_history_total'];
    52	                $point = $pointDifferential['point'];
    53	
    54	                $player->setPoint($pointHistoryTotal);
    55	                $this->entityManager->persist($player);
    56	
    57	                $pointVarianceList[] = [
    58	                    'customerId' => $customerId,
    59	                    'pointHistoryTotal' => $pointHistoryTotal,
    60	                    'point' => $point,
    61	                    'pointVariance' => $point - $pointHistoryTotal,
    62	                ];
    63	            }
    64	
    65	            $this->entityManager->flush();
    66	            $this->entityManager->commit();
    67	        } catch (\Throwable $e) {
    68	            $this->entityManager->rollback();
    69	
    70	            throw $e;
    71	        }
    72	
    73	        $this->mailService->sendAdjustPointVarianceMail($pointVarianceList);
    74	    }
    75	}

     1	<?php
     2	
     3	declare(strict_types=1);
     4	
     5	/*
     6	 * This file is part of EC-CUBE
     7	 *
     8	 * Copyright(c) EC-CUBE CO.,LTD. All Rights Reserved.
     9	 *
    10	 * http://www.ec-cube.co.jp/

    13	 * file that was distributed with this source code.
    14	 */
    15	
    16	namespace Eccube\MessageHandler;
    17	
    18	use Doctrine\ORM\EntityManagerInterface;
    19	use Eccube\Entity\MessengerJob;
    20	use Eccube\Message\SmaregiCustomerPointAddMessage;
    21	use Eccube\Repository\DtbPointHistoryRepository;
    22	use Eccube\Repository\MessengerJobRepository;
    23	use Eccube\Service\Smaregi\Api\SmaregiMessengerJobContext;
    24	use Eccube\Service\Smaregi\SmaregiCustomerService;
    25	use Eccube\Service\Smaregi\SmaregiMessengerJobProcessingLock;
    26	use Eccube\Service\Smaregi\SmaregiPointPushLogService;
    27	use Psr\Log\LoggerInterface;
    28	use Symfony\Component\Messenger\Attribute\AsMessageHandler;
    29	
    30	/**
    31	 * 管理画面ポイント付与をスマレジ会員ポイントへ反映する非同期ハンドラ.
    32	 *
    33	 * MessengerJob 行を悲観ロックして重複実行を防ぎ、PENDING/FAILED のときのみ PROCESSING へ遷移する。
    34	 * 失敗時は FAILED にして例外を再送出し、Messenger のリトライに委ねる。
    35	 */
    36	#[AsMessageHandler]
    37	final readonly class SmaregiCustomerPointAddMessageHandler
    38	{
    39	    public function __construct(
    40	        private LoggerInterface $logger,
    41	        private EntityManagerInterface $entityManager,
    42	        private SmaregiMessengerJobProcessingLock $smaregiMessengerJobProcessingLock,
    43	        private MessengerJobRepository $messengerJobRepository,
    44	        private DtbPointHistoryRepository $pointHistoryRepository,
    45	        private SmaregiCustomerService $smaregiCustomerService,
    46	        private SmaregiMessengerJobContext $smaregiMessengerJobContext,
    47	        private SmaregiPointPushLogService $smaregiPointPushLogService,
    48	    ) {
    49	    }
    50	
    51	    public function __invoke(SmaregiCustomerPointAddMessage $message): void
    52	    {
    53	        $Job = $this->messengerJobRepository->find($message->getJobId());
    54	        if (!$Job instanceof MessengerJob) {
    55	            $this->logger->error('MessengerJob not found for SmaregiCustomerPointAddMessage', [
    56	                'jobId' => $message->getJobId(),
    57	                'pointHistoryId' => $message->getPointHistoryId(),
    58	            ]);
    59	
    60	            return;
    61	        }
    62	
    63	        $this->smaregiMessengerJobContext->setJobId((int) $Job->getId());
    64	        try {
    65	            if (!$this->smaregiMessengerJobProcessingLock->tryBeginProcessingJob($Job)) {
    66	                return;
    67	            }
    68	
    69	            $pointHistory = $this->pointHistoryRepository->find($message->getPointHistoryId());
    70	            if ($pointHistory === null) {
    71	                $this->failJob($Job, 'ポイント履歴が見つかりません (pointHistoryId='.$message->getPointHistoryId().').');
    72	
    73	                return;
    74	            }
    75	
    76	            $smaregiId = $pointHistory->getCustomer()->getPlayer()?->getSmaregiId();
    77	            if ($smaregiId === null || $smaregiId === '') {
    78	                $this->completeJobSkipped($Job, 'スマレジ未連携の会員のためポイント連携をスキップしました。');
    79	
    80	                return;
    81	            }
    82	
    83	            $pointChange = $pointHistory->getPointChange();
    84	            if ($pointChange === null || $pointChange === 0) {
    85	                $this->completeJobSkipped($Job, '増減ポイントが 0 のためポイント連携をスキップしました。');
    86	
    87	                return;
    88	            }
    89	
    90	            try {
    91	                $response = $this->smaregiCustomerService->postSmaregiPoint($smaregiId, $pointChange);
    92	            } catch (\Throwable $e) {
    93	                // postSmaregiPoint は通常例外を投げないが、想定外例外は適用有無が不明なため自動リトライせず手動照合へ回す。
    94	                $this->failJob($Job, 'スマレジ会員ポイント更新で想定外の例外が発生しました（適用有無不明・要手動照合）: '.$e->getMessage());
    95	
    96	                return;
    97	            }
    98	
    99	            if (!array_key_exists('result', $response)) {
   100	                $error = isset($response['error']) && is_string($response['error']) ? $response['error'] : '';
   101	
   102	                // ポイント加算API(point/add)は相対加算で非冪等。加算API実行後（statusCode あり）の失敗は
   103	                // 適用済みの可能性があり、自動リトライすると二重加算になりうるため再送出せず FAILED にして手動照合へ回す。
   104	                if (array_key_exists('statusCode', $response)) {
   105	                    $this->failJob($Job, 'スマレジ会員ポイント更新が不確定な状態で失敗しました（自動リトライ不可・要手動照合）: '.$error);
   106	
   107	                    return;
   108	                }
   109	
   110	                // 加算API実行前の失敗（トークン取得 / customerId 解決）＝外部未適用が確定しているため再送出して再試行する。
   111	                $this->failJob($Job, 'スマレジ会員ポイント更新に失敗しました（外部未適用・再試行）: '.$error);
   112	
   113	                throw new \RuntimeException('スマレジ会員ポイント更新に失敗しました（外部未適用・再試行）: '.$error);
   114	            }
   115	
   116	            // ここに到達した時点でスマレジ側のポイント加算は成功している。
   117	            // echo 抑止の送信記録(persist)と COMPLETED を同一 flush で確定する。ここでの「失敗」は DB 永続化(flush)の失敗を指し、
   118	            // その場合はどちらも残さない（原子的）。取引ID無しは失敗ではない（成功で取引ID無しは postSmaregiPoint でエラー化、skipped は取引なしで記録不要）。
   119	            // point/add は非冪等のため、この flush 失敗では再送出（リトライ）せずログのみとする（echo 抑止が DB に残らない恐れ・要手動照合）。
   120	            try {
   121	                // この point/add がスマレジ側に生む取引が Webhook で戻ってきても二重処理しないよう、取引IDを送信記録へ積む。
   122	                $this->smaregiPointPushLogService->recordFromResult($response);
   123	                $Job->setStatus(MessengerJob::STATUS_COMPLETED);
   124	                $Job->setCompletedAt(new \DateTime());
   125	                $Job->setErrorMessage(null);
   126	                $this->entityManager->flush();
   127	            } catch (\Throwable $persistError) {
   128	                $this->logger->error('Smaregi customer point applied but failed to persist push log/COMPLETED state', [
   129	                    'jobId' => $Job->getId(),
   130	                    'error' => $persistError->getMessage(),
   131	                ]);
   132	            }
   133	        } finally {
   134	            $this->smaregiMessengerJobContext->clear();
   135	        }
   136	    }
   137	
   138	    private function failJob(MessengerJob $Job, string $errorMessage): void
   139	    {
   140	        $Job->setStatus(MessengerJob::STATUS_FAILED);
   141	        $Job->setCompletedAt(new \DateTime());
   142	        $Job->setErrorMessage($errorMessage);
   143	        try {
   144	            $this->entityManager->flush();
   145	        } catch (\Throwable $persistError) {
   146	            $this->logger->error('Failed to persist Smaregi customer point job failure state', [
   147	                'jobId' => $Job->getId(),
   148	                'error' => $persistError->getMessage(),
   149	            ]);
   150	        }
   151	        $this->logger->error($errorMessage, ['jobId' => $Job->getId()]);
   152	    }
   153	
   154	    private function completeJobSkipped(MessengerJob $Job, string $reason): void
   155	    {
   156	        $Job->setStatus(MessengerJob::STATUS_COMPLETED);
   157	        $Job->setCompletedAt(new \DateTime());
   158	        $Job->setErrorMessage(null);
   159	        try {
   160	            $this->entityManager->flush();
   161	        } catch (\Throwable $persistError) {
   162	            $this->logger->error('Failed to persist Smaregi customer point job skipped state', [
   163	                'jobId' => $Job->getId(),
   164	                'error' => $persistError->getMessage(),
   165	            ]);
   166	        }
   167	        $this->logger->info($reason, ['jobId' => $Job->getId()]);
   168	    }
   169	}

    73	    public function newCustomer(): Customer
    74	    {
    75	        $CustomerGroup = $this->getEntityManager()
    76	            ->find(DtbCustomerGroup::class, DtbCustomerGroup::NORMAL);
    77	
    78	        $IdentityConfirmStatus = $this->getEntityManager()
    79	            ->find(MtbIdentityConfirmStatus::class, MtbIdentityConfirmStatus::STATUS_UNCONFIRMED);
    80	
    81	        $Player = new DtbPlayer();
    82	        $Player
    83	            ->setMailMagazineFlg(DtbPlayer::MAIL_MAGAZINE_DISALLOW)
    84	            ->setIdentityConfirmStatus($IdentityConfirmStatus)
    85	            ->setCustomerGroup($CustomerGroup)
    86	        ;
    87	
    88	        $Player = $this->playerService->setNewDeckUserId($Player, $this->eccubeConfig['eccube_deck_user_id_len']);
    89	
    90	        $CustomerStatus = $this->getEntityManager()
    91	            ->find(CustomerStatus::class, CustomerStatus::PROVISIONAL);
    92	
    93	        $Customer = new Customer();
    94	        $Customer
    95	            ->setStatus($CustomerStatus)
    96	            ->setPoint('0')
    97	            ->setPlayer($Player)
    98	        ;
    99	
   100	        $Player->setCustomer($Customer);
   101	
   102	        return $Customer;
   103	    }

   150	    public function getQueryBuilderBySearchData(array $searchData): QueryBuilder
   151	    {
   152	        $qb = $this->createQueryBuilder('c')
   153	            ->select('c')
   154	        ;
   155	
   156	        if (isset($searchData['multi']) && StringUtil::isNotBlank($searchData['multi'])) {
   157	            // スペース除去
   158	            $clean_key_multi = preg_replace('/\s+|[　]+/u', '', (string) $searchData['multi']);
   159	            $id = preg_match('/^\d{0,10}$/', (string) $clean_key_multi) ? $clean_key_multi : null;
   160	            if ($id && $id > '2147483647' && $this->isPostgreSQL()) {
   161	                $id = null;
   162	            }
   163	            $qb
   164	                ->andWhere("c.id = :customer_id OR CONCAT(c.name01, c.name02) LIKE :name OR CONCAT(COALESCE(c.kana01, ''), COALESCE(c.kana02, '')) LIKE :kana OR c.email LIKE :email")
   165	                ->setParameter('customer_id', $id)
   166	                ->setParameter('name', '%'.$clean_key_multi.'%')
   167	                ->setParameter('kana', '%'.$clean_key_multi.'%')
   168	                ->setParameter('email', '%'.$clean_key_multi.'%');
   169	        }
   170	
   171	        // Pref
   172	        if (isset($searchData['prefMulti']) && StringUtil::isNotBlank($searchData['prefMulti'])) {
   173	            $prefs = [];
   174	            foreach ($searchData['prefMulti'] as $pref) {
   175	                $prefs[] = $pref->getId();
   176	            }
   177	
   178	            $qb
   179	                ->andWhere($qb->expr()->in('c.Pref', ':prefs'))
   180	                ->setParameter('prefs', $prefs);

  1017	    public function getHtmlTemplate(string $templateName): ?string
  1018	    {
  1019	        // メールテンプレート名からHTMLメール用テンプレート名を生成
  1020	        $fileName = explode('.', $templateName);
  1021	        $suffix = '.html';
  1022	        $htmlFileName = $fileName[0].$suffix.'.'.$fileName[1];

  1031	
  1032	    /**
  1033	     * RFC違反のメールの local part を "" で囲む.
  1034	     *
  1035	     * パラメータ eccube_rfc_email_check == true の場合は変換しない
  1036	     */
  1037	    public function convertRFCViolatingEmail(string $email): Address
  1038	    {
  1039	        if ($this->eccubeConfig->get('eccube_rfc_email_check')) {
  1040	            return new Address($email);
  1041	        }
  1042	
  1043	        $wsp = '[\x20\x09]';
  1044	        $vchar = '[\x21-\x7e]';
  1045	        $quoted_pair = "\\\\(?:$vchar|$wsp)";
  1046	        $qtext = '[\x21\x23-\x5b\x5d-\x7e]';
  1047	        $qcontent = "(?:$qtext|$quoted_pair)";
  1048	        $quoted_string = "\"$qcontent*\"";
  1049	        $atext = '[a-zA-Z0-9!#$%&\'*+\-\/\=?^_`{|}~]';
  1050	        $dot_atom = "$atext+(?:[.]$atext+)*";
  1051	        $local_part = "(?:$dot_atom|$quoted_string)";
  1052	        $domain = $dot_atom;
  1053	        $addr_spec = "{$local_part}[@]$domain";
  1054	
  1055	        $regexp = "/\A{$addr_spec}\z/";
  1056	        if (!preg_match($regexp, $email)) {
  1057	            $email = preg_replace('/^(.*)@(.*)$/', '"$1"@$2', $email);
  1058	        }

    45	     * 会員一括メール送信
    46	     *
    47	     * @return array<string, mixed>|Response
    48	     *
    49	     * @throws ORMException
    50	     * @throws OptimisticLockException
    51	     */
    52	    #[Route(path: '/%eccube_admin_route%/customer/mail', name: 'admin_customer_mail', methods: ['POST'])]
    53	    #[Template(template: '@admin/Customer/mail.twig')]
    54	    public function index(Request $request): array|Response
    55	    {
    56	        if (is_array($request->get('ids'))) {
    57	            $customerIds = implode(',', $request->get('ids'));
    58	        } else {
    59	            $customerIds = $request->get('ids');
    60	        }
    61	
    62	        $builder = $this->formFactory->createBuilder(
    63	            CustomerMailType::class,
    64	            null,
    65	            ['ids' => $customerIds]
    66	        );
    67	
    68	        $event = new EventArgs(
    69	            [
    70	                'builder' => $builder,
    71	            ],
    72	            $request
    73	        );
    74	        $this->eventDispatcher->dispatch($event, EccubeEvents::ADMIN_CUSTOMER_MAIL_INDEX_INITIALIZE);
    75	
    76	        $form = $builder->getForm();
    77	
    78	        if ($request->getMethod() === 'POST') {
    79	            $form->handleRequest($request);
    80	            $mode = $request->get('mode');
    81	
    82	            switch ($mode) {
    83	                case 'confirm':
    84	                    if ($form->isSubmitted() && $form->isValid()) {
    85	                        // 会員の検索
    86	                        $Customers = $this->customerRepository->findByIds($form->get('ids')->getData());
    87	
    88	                        $event = new EventArgs(
    89	                            [
    90	                                'form' => $form,
    91	                                'Customers' => $Customers,
    92	                            ],
    93	                            $request
    94	                        );
    95	                        $this->eventDispatcher->dispatch($event, EccubeEvents::ADMIN_CUSTOMER_MAIL_INDEX_CONFIRM);
    96	
    97	                        return $this->render('@admin/Customer/mail_confirm.twig', [
    98	                            'form' => $form->createView(),
    99	                            'Customers' => $Customers,
   100	                        ]);
   101	                    }
   102	                    break;
   103	                case 'complete':
   104	                    if ($form->isSubmitted() && $form->isValid()) {
   105	                        $formParams = $form->getData();
   106	
   107	                        // 会員の検索
   108	                        $Customers = $this->customerRepository->findByIds($formParams['ids']);
   109	
   110	                        foreach ($Customers as $Customer) {
   111	                            // メール送信
   112	                            $message = $this->mailService->sendCustomerMail($Customer, $formParams);
   113	
   114	                            // メール履歴作成
   115	                            $this->mailHistoryEntityManager->save($message, $this->getMember()->getBaseInfo(), $Customer, null);
   116	                            $this->entityManager->flush();
   117	                        }
   118	
   119	                        $event = new EventArgs(
   120	                            [
   121	                                'form' => $form,
   122	                                'Customers' => $Customers,
   123	                            ],
   124	                            $request
   125	                        );
   126	                        $this->eventDispatcher->dispatch($event, EccubeEvents::ADMIN_CUSTOMER_MAIL_INDEX_COMPLETE);
   127	
   128	                        return $this->render('@admin/Customer/mail_complete.twig', [
   129	                        ]);
   130	                    }
   131	                    break;
   132	                default:
   133	                    break;
   134	            }
   135	        }
   136	
   137	        return [
   138	            'form' => $form->createView(),
   139	        ];
   140	    }
   141	
   142	    /**
   143	     * @return array<string, mixed>
   144	     */
   145	    #[Route(path: '/%eccube_admin_route%/customer/mail/{id}/history', name: 'admin_customer_mail_history', requirements: ['id' => '\d+'], methods: ['GET'])]
   146	    #[Template(template: '@admin/Customer/mail_history.twig')]
   147	    public function history(Request $request, Customer $Customer): array
   148	    {
   149	        $builder = $this->formFactory->createBuilder(CustomerMailHistoryType::class);
   150	
   151	        $event = new EventArgs(
   152	            [
   153	                'builder' => $builder,
   154	                'Customer' => $Customer,
   155	            ],
   156	            $request
   157	        );
   158	        $this->eventDispatcher->dispatch($event, EccubeEvents::ADMIN_CUSTOMER_MAIL_HISTORY_INITIALIZE);
   159	
   160	        $searchForm = $builder->getForm();
   161	        $searchForm->handleRequest($request);
   162	
   163	        $searchData = $searchForm->getData();
   164	
   165	        $criteria = [
   166	            'Customer' => $Customer,
   167	        ];
   168	
   169	        if (is_array($searchData) && !empty($searchData['mailTemplate'])) {
   170	            $criteria['MailTemplate'] = $searchData['mailTemplate'];
   171	        }
   172	
   173	        $MailHistories = $this->mailHistoryRepository->findBy(
   174	            $criteria,
   175	            ['send_date' => 'DESC']
   176	        );
   177	
   178	        return [
   179	            'Customer' => $Customer,
   180	            'MailHistories' => $MailHistories,
   181	            'form' => $searchForm->createView(),
   182	        ];
   183	    }
   184	
   185	    /**
   186	     * @return array<string, mixed>|Response
   187	     */
   188	    #[Route(path: '/%eccube_admin_route%/customer/manual_mail/{id}/{template_id}', name: 'admin_customer_manual_mail', requirements: ['id' => '\d+', 'template_id' => '\d+|null'], defaults: ['template_id' => null], methods: ['GET', 'POST'])]
   189	    #[Template(template: '@admin/Customer/mail_manual.twig')]
   190	    public function manual_mail(Request $request, Customer $Customer, #[MapEntity(id: 'template_id')] ?MailTemplate $MailTemplate): array|Response
   191	    {
   192	        $builder = $this->formFactory->createBuilder(
   193	            CustomerManualMailType::class,
   194	            null,
   195	            [
   196	                'MailTemplate' => $MailTemplate,
   197	                'autoMail' => false,
   198	                'baseFiles' => [MailTemplate::BASE_CUSTOMER, MailTemplate::BASE_CUSTOMER_EN],
   199	            ]
   200	        );
   201	
   202	        $event = new EventArgs(
   203	            [
   204	                'builder' => $builder,
   205	                'Customer' => $Customer,
   206	                'MailTemplate' => $MailTemplate,
   207	            ],
   208	            $request
   209	        );
   210	        $this->eventDispatcher->dispatch($event, EccubeEvents::ADMIN_CUSTOMER_MAIL_MANUAL_MAIL_INITIALIZE);
   211	
   212	        $form = $builder->getForm();
   213	        if ($MailTemplate) {
   214	            $body = $this->renderView(
   215	                $MailTemplate->getFileName(),
   216	                [
   217	                    'Customer' => $Customer,
   218	                    'header' => $MailTemplate->getHeader(),
   219	                    'footer' => $MailTemplate->getFooter(),
   220	                ]
   221	            );
   222	
   223	            $form->get('mail_subject')->setData($MailTemplate->getMailSubject());
   224	            $form->get('body')->setData($body);
   225	
   226	            $event = new EventArgs(
   227	                [
   228	                    'form' => $form,
   229	                    'Customer' => $Customer,
   230	                    'MailTemplate' => $MailTemplate,
   231	                ],
   232	                $request
   233	            );
   234	            $this->eventDispatcher->dispatch($event, EccubeEvents::ADMIN_CUSTOMER_MAIL_MANUAL_MAIL_CHANGE);
   235	        }
   236	
   237	        if ($request->getMethod() === 'POST') {
   238	            $form->handleRequest($request);
   239	            $mode = $request->get('mode');
   240	
   241	            if ($form->isSubmitted() && $form->isValid()) {
   242	                switch ($mode) {
   243	                    case 'confirm':
   244	                        $event = new EventArgs(
   245	                            [
   246	                                'form' => $form,
   247	                                'Customer' => $Customer,
   248	                                'MailTemplate' => $MailTemplate,
   249	                            ],
   250	                            $request
   251	                        );
   252	                        $this->eventDispatcher->dispatch($event, EccubeEvents::ADMIN_CUSTOMER_MAIL_MANUAL_MAIL_CONFIRM);
   253	
   254	                        return $this->render('@admin/Customer/mail_manual_confirm.twig', [
   255	                            'form' => $form->createView(),
   256	                            'MailTemplate' => $MailTemplate,
   257	                            'Customer' => $Customer,
   258	                        ]);
   259	                    case 'complete':
   260	                        $formParams = $form->getData();
   261	
   262	                        // メール送信
   263	                        $message = $this->mailService->sendCustomerManualMail($Customer, $formParams);
   264	
   265	                        // メール履歴作成
   266	                        $this->mailHistoryEntityManager->save($message, $this->getMember()->getBaseInfo(), $Customer, $MailTemplate);
   267	                        $this->entityManager->flush();
   268	
   269	                        $event = new EventArgs(
   270	                            [
   271	                                'form' => $form,
   272	                                'Customer' => $Customer,
   273	                                'MailTemplate' => $MailTemplate,
   274	                            ],
   275	                            $request
   276	                        );
   277	                        $this->eventDispatcher->dispatch($event, EccubeEvents::ADMIN_CUSTOMER_MAIL_MANUAL_MAIL_COMPLETE);
   278	
   279	                        $this->addSuccess('admin.customer.manual_mail.success', 'admin');
   280	
   281	                        return $this->redirectToRoute('admin_customer_edit', ['id' => $Customer->getId()]);
   282	                    case 'back':
   283	                        return $this->redirectToRoute('admin_customer_edit', ['id' => $Customer->getId()]);
   284	                    default:
   285	                        break;
   286	                }
   287	            }
   288	        }
   289	
   290	        return [
   291	            'Customer' => $Customer,
   292	            'MailTemplate' => $MailTemplate,
   293	            'form' => $form->createView(),
   294	        ];
   295	    }

    37	        }

    52	                        ->setParameter('autoSend', $options['autoMail']);

    56	                            ->setParameter('fileNames', $fileNames);
    57	                    }
    58	
    59	                    return $qb->orderBy('mt.name', 'ASC');
    60	                },
    61	            ])
    62	            ->add('mail_subject', TextType::class, [
    63	                'label' => 'タイトル',
    64	                'mapped' => true,

    66	                'constraints' => [
    67	                    new NotBlank(),
    68	                ],
    69	            ])
    70	            ->add('body', TextareaType::class, [
    71	                'label' => false,
    72	                'mapped' => true,
    73	                'required' => false,
    74	                'constraints' => [
    75	                    new NotBlank(),
    76	                    new TwigLint(),
    77	                ],
    78	            ])
    79	        ;
    80	    }

    18	use Doctrine\ORM\EntityManagerInterface;
    19	use Eccube\Entity\BaseInfo;
    20	use Eccube\Entity\Customer;
    21	use Eccube\Entity\DtbBuyOrder;
    22	use Eccube\Entity\MailHistory;
    23	use Eccube\Entity\MailTemplate;
    24	use Eccube\Entity\Order;
    25	use Symfony\Component\Mime\Email;
    26	
    27	class MailHistoryEntityManager
    28	{
    29	    public function __construct(private readonly EntityManagerInterface $entityManager)
    30	    {
    31	    }
    32	
    33	    public function save(
    34	        Email $Message,
    35	        BaseInfo $BaseInfo,
    36	        Customer|Order|DtbBuyOrder|null $ContextInfo = null,
    37	        ?MailTemplate $MailTemplate = null,
    38	    ): void {
    39	        $MailHistory = new MailHistory();
    40	        $MailHistory
    41	            ->setMailSubject($Message->getSubject())
    42	            ->setBaseInfo($BaseInfo)
    43	            ->setMailBody($Message->getTextBody())
    44	            ->setSendDate(new \DateTime());
    45	
    46	        $htmlBody = $Message->getHtmlBody();
    47	        if (!empty($htmlBody)) {
    48	            $MailHistory->setMailHtmlBody($htmlBody);
    49	        }
    50	
    51	        if ($MailTemplate !== null) {
    52	            $MailHistory->setMailTemplate($MailTemplate);
    53	        }
    54	
    55	        if ($ContextInfo instanceof Customer) {
    56	            $MailHistory->setCustomer($ContextInfo);
    57	        } elseif ($ContextInfo instanceof Order) {
    58	            $MailHistory
    59	                ->setOrder($ContextInfo)
    60	                ->setCustomer($ContextInfo->getCustomer());
    61	        } elseif ($ContextInfo instanceof DtbBuyOrder) {
    62	            $MailHistory
    63	                ->setBuyOrder($ContextInfo)
    64	                ->setCustomer($ContextInfo->getCustomer());
    65	        }
    66	
    67	        $this->entityManager->persist($MailHistory);
    68	    }
    69	}

    62	     *
    63	     * @group HARERUYA
    64	     */
    65	    public function testHandleAppliesExpiryAndDispatchesSmaregiJob(): void
    66	    {
    67	        $Customer = $this->generator->createCustomer();
    68	        $Player = $this->generator->createPlayer($Customer);
    69	        $Player->setSmaregiId('SM12345678')
    70	            ->setPoint(150);
    71	        $this->entityManager->flush();
    72	
    73	        // 有効期限切れのポイント履歴（失効対象）
    74	        $ExpiredHistory = new DtbPointHistory();
    75	        $ExpiredHistory->setCustomer($Customer)
    76	            ->setPointChange(100)
    77	            ->setIssueDate((new \DateTime())->modify('-400 days'))
    78	            ->setNote('Expired point');
    79	        $this->entityManager->persist($ExpiredHistory);
    80	
    81	        // 有効期限内のポイント履歴（残存）
    82	        $ValidHistory = new DtbPointHistory();
    83	        $ValidHistory->setCustomer($Customer)
    84	            ->setPointChange(50)
    85	            ->setIssueDate((new \DateTime())->modify('-10 days'))
    86	            ->setNote('Valid point');
    87	        $this->entityManager->persist($ValidHistory);
    88	
    89	        $this->entityManager->flush();
    90	
    91	        $PointType = $this->pointTypeRepository->find(MtbPointType::GRANTED_TYPE);
    92	        $this->assertNotNull($PointType, 'PointType must exist');
    93	
    94	        $Job = $this->createMock(MessengerJob::class);
    95	        $eventService = $this->createMock(SmaregiCustomerPointEventService::class);
    96	        $eventService->expects($this->once())
    97	            ->method('registerPointAddJob')
    98	            ->willReturn($Job);
    99	        $eventService->expects($this->once())
   100	            ->method('dispatchPointAddMessage')
   101	            ->with($Job, $this->isInstanceOf(DtbPointHistory::class));
   102	
   103	        $beforeCount = $this->entityManager->getRepository(DtbPointHistory::class)->count([]);
   104	
   105	        $this->createAction($eventService)->handle();
   106	
   107	        $this->entityManager->refresh($Player);
   108	        $this->assertSame(50, $Player->getPoint());
   109	
   110	        $afterCount = $this->entityManager->getRepository(DtbPointHistory::class)->count([]);
   111	        $this->assertSame($beforeCount + 1, $afterCount);
   112	
   113	        $savedHistories = $this->entityManager->getRepository(DtbPointHistory::class)
   114	            ->findBy(['Customer' => $Customer], ['id' => 'DESC']);
   115	        $NewestHistory = $savedHistories[0];
   116	        $this->assertSame(-100, $NewestHistory->getPointChange());
   117	        $this->assertSame('有効期限切れによる失効', $NewestHistory->getNote());
   118	        $this->assertSame($PointType->getId(), $NewestHistory->getPointType()->getId());
   119	    }
   120	
   121	    /**
   122	     * handle - 正常系
   123	     * 複数の失効対象会員をそれぞれ会員単位で失効させ、各会員ごとに連携ジョブ登録と dispatch を行うこと
   124	     *
   125	     * @group HARERUYA
   126	     */
   127	    public function testHandleProcessesEachTargetPlayer(): void
   128	    {
   129	        $Customer1 = $this->generator->createCustomer();
   130	        $Player1 = $this->generator->createPlayer($Customer1);
   131	        $Player1->setSmaregiId('SM11111111')
   132	            ->setPoint(150);
   133	
   134	        $Customer2 = $this->generator->createCustomer();
   135	        $Player2 = $this->generator->createPlayer($Customer2);
   136	        $Player2->setSmaregiId('SM22222222')
   137	            ->setPoint(100);
   138	        $this->entityManager->flush();
   139	
   140	        // Player1: 失効100 + 残存50 → 残ポイント50
   141	        $Expired1 = new DtbPointHistory();
   142	        $Expired1->setCustomer($Customer1)
   143	            ->setPointChange(100)
   144	            ->setIssueDate((new \DateTime())->modify('-400 days'))
   145	            ->setNote('Expired point');
   146	        $this->entityManager->persist($Expired1);
   147

   149	        $Valid1->setCustomer($Customer1)
   150	            ->setPointChange(50)
   151	            ->setIssueDate((new \DateTime())->modify('-10 days'))
   152	            ->setNote('Valid point');
   153	        $this->entityManager->persist($Valid1);
   154	
   155	        // Player2: 失効100 → 残ポイント0
   156	        $Expired2 = new DtbPointHistory();
   157	        $Expired2->setCustomer($Customer2)
   158	            ->setPointChange(100)
   159	            ->setIssueDate((new \DateTime())->modify('-400 days'))
   160	            ->setNote('Expired point');
   161	        $this->entityManager->persist($Expired2);
   162	
   163	        $this->entityManager->flush();
   164	
   165	        $Job = $this->createMock(MessengerJob::class);
   166	        $eventService = $this->createMock(SmaregiCustomerPointEventService::class);
   167	        $eventService->expects($this->exactly(2))
   168	            ->method('registerPointAddJob')
   169	            ->willReturn($Job);
   170	        $eventService->expects($this->exactly(2))
   171	            ->method('dispatchPointAddMessage')
   172	            ->with($Job, $this->isInstanceOf(DtbPointHistory::class));
   173	
   174	        $this->createAction($eventService)->handle();
   175	
   176	        $this->entityManager->refresh($Player1);
   177	        $this->entityManager->refresh($Player2);
   178	        $this->assertSame(50, $Player1->getPoint());
   179	        $this->assertSame(0, $Player2->getPoint());
   180	    }

    41	    /**
    42	     * 指定したスマレジ取引IDのポイント履歴が既に存在するか判定する.
    43	     *
    44	     * スマレジ取引由来のポイント付与の冪等性担保 (Webhook 再送・補完取得バッチでの重複付与防止) に使う。
    45	     */
    46	    public function existsByTransactionId(int $transactionId): bool
    47	    {
    48	        $count = (int) $this->createQueryBuilder('ph')
    49	            ->select('COUNT(ph.id)')
    50	            ->where('ph.transactionId = :transactionId')
    51	            ->setParameter('transactionId', $transactionId)
    52	            ->getQuery()
    53	            ->getSingleScalarResult();
    54	
    55	        return $count > 0;
    56	    }
    57	
    58	    /**
    59	     * 指定したスマレジ取引IDのうち、ポイント履歴が既に存在するものだけを返す.
    60	     *
    61	     * Webhook 連携エラー再連携バッチ ({@see \Eccube\Service\Smaregi\Webhook\Transaction\SmaregiTransactionBackfillAction})
    62	     * が取引一覧の 1 ページ分をまとめてポイント反映済み判定するために使う
    63	     * ({@see existsByTransactionId()} を件数分呼ぶと N+1 になるため).
    64	     *
    65	     * @param list<int> $transactionIds
    66	     *
    67	     * @return list<int>
    68	     */
    69	    public function findExistingTransactionIdsIn(array $transactionIds): array
    70	    {
    71	        if ($transactionIds === []) {
    72	            return [];
    73	        }
    74	
    75	        /** @var list<array{transactionId:int}> $rows */
    76	        $rows = $this->createQueryBuilder('ph')
    77	            ->select('DISTINCT ph.transactionId AS transactionId')
    78	            ->where('ph.transactionId IN (:transactionIds)')
    79	            ->setParameter('transactionIds', $transactionIds)
    80	            ->getQuery()
    81	            ->getResult();
    82	
    83	        return array_map(static fn (array $row): int => (int) $row['transactionId'], $rows);
    84	    }
    85	
    86	    /**
    87	     * 指定したスマレジ取引IDの「ポイント専用取引」(受注に紐付かない = order_id が NULL) のポイント履歴を取得する.
    88	     *
    89	     * 受注由来 (order_id 有り) のポイント履歴と区別し、ポイント専用取引 (取引区分6/7) の
    90	     * 取消/打消による差し戻し対象のみを抽出するために使う。
    91	     *
    92	     * @return list<DtbPointHistory>
    93	     */
    94	    public function findPointOnlyByTransactionId(int $transactionId): array
    95	    {
    96	        /** @var list<DtbPointHistory> $result */
    97	        $result = $this->createQueryBuilder('ph')
    98	            ->where('ph.transactionId = :transactionId')
    99	            ->andWhere('ph.Order IS NULL')
   100	            ->setParameter('transactionId', $transactionId)
   101	            ->orderBy('ph.id', 'ASC')
   102	            ->getQuery()
   103	            ->getResult();
   104	
   105	        return $result;
   106	    }

   111	     * 商品明細を伴う売上取引の取消/打消でポイントを差し戻す際、EC 側オンライン購入時のポイント履歴
